13Assumptions, dependencies, risks and open decisions

13.1Assumptions

  • The existing platform modules expose APIs and events sufficient for section 9 (listed in 9.10); SynapseDC consumes them and builds nothing of the modules themselves. Their teams own the contracts and agree the fields SynapseDC needs.
  • The OAP telemetry ingestion and consumer microservices are available, read and write ClickHouse, and carry the sample rates in NFR-003.
  • Customers grant read-only access to the BMS, UPS, PDUs, network, BMCs and (Neo) DCGM / scheduler, and raise the firewall rules in the interface specification.
  • One collector (plus standby) can be placed on each site's management network.
  • Service placement (which device / port / rack carries which service) exists in BSS Orders, the OSS / control plane or the tenant register; without it impact and revenue are per site only.

13.2Dependencies

OnFor
OAP telemetry ingestion / consumer services (ClickHouse)every live figure, history and freshness (P1)
Ops Toolkit System Alertsalarm record, routing, paging (P1)
Services & ITSM Incidents / Change Managementincident and change records, CR gate for DCIM (P1)
BSS Customers / Orders / Catalogcustomers, contracts, services (P1)
Plan to Build Inventoryasset record for DCIM extensions (P1)
Platform Engineering identityOIDC, roles, scopes (P1)
Plan to Build Programs & Projectsplanner hand-off, delivery analytics (P2)
Ops Toolkit Security Opsdetection store, rules, cases (P2)
AI FoundryOpstral AI copilot (P2)

13.3Risks

IDRiskImpactMitigation
R-01BMS owners refuse BACnet access (safety)Environment / cooling tabs partly blindRelay contacts and OPC UA alternatives; the site survey settles it before go-live
R-02GPU job progress (step time, MFU, checkpoints) is only inside the training frameworkGPU time lost and work-to-redo degrade to allocation-levelAgree a metrics / log export with the AI platform team; show scheduler-level state until then
R-03Service placement missing or staleImpact and money per customer wrongPlacement differences reported to BSS / OSS; site-level figures flagged "placement incomplete"
R-04Platform module APIs not ready for the fields neededIntegration slipsAPI needs in 9.10 agreed per module before P1 build; gaps go to the module's backlog (ARCH-004); the import template loads only datacenter-specific data
R-05Power chain and cabling records absent at most customersPhysical last mile, SPOF and what-if emptyTemplate, site survey and audit services at onboarding; DCIM lets engineers record as they audit
R-06Tariffs and SLA credit rules differ per contractMoney figures disputedContract-level rules synced from BSS; "illustrative" flag until replaced
R-07Tenant privacy concerns (Colo)Sales objectionNever poll tenant gear; cabinet-level only; clear per-model wording in the pitch

15Glossary

TermMeaning
Alarm scopesite / edge / dependency / downstream: whether an alarm counts against a datacenter (DD-01)
Blast radiusthe GPUs, nodes and jobs stopped by one fault
Block (power)a colocation power contract: committed kW billed per month
CDUcoolant distribution unit for liquid-cooled racks
Change request (CR)a Change Management record; every DCIM write runs under one (standard / open / new)
Collectorthe on-site software that reads the equipment and sends outbound to the platform
DCGMNVIDIA Data Center GPU Manager; the per-GPU telemetry field set
Exposed (service)active but riding a Critical fault (UPS on battery, failed CRAH, critical alarm on its path)
GPU time loststalled job × all its GPUs + slowed job × step-time share + idle allocation × all GPUs, at the GPU rate
MFUmodel FLOPs utilisation of a training job
Opstralthe platform's Ops Toolkit and AI copilot
Persona / business modelnetwork, colocation, cloud, enterprise, NeoCloud — one estate, different vocabulary, monitoring and money
Plan pack / review packthe planner's generated document set (PDF, workbook, drawings, CSV) for approval
Ride-throughminutes a site survives a utility loss: UPS → DG start → fuel hours
SPOFsingle point of failure: a cord pair on one PDU side, one-side cabinet, Tier II single feed, lost cooling spare…
Take-or-pay floorthe minimum share of the utility connection billed whether drawn or not
Tenant gearcolocation customer equipment: an opaque block per cabinet, never polled
Work to redoGPUs × time since the job's last checkpoint
XidNVIDIA GPU error code; 48 / 64 / 79 / 95 = reset or restart, 74 = NVLink, 94 = contained

Review checklist for approvers: the module split in section 3.2 is right for your module; the data SynapseDC reads from and sends to your module (section 9) exists or can be provided; the open decisions in 13.4 that name you have an owner and a date; the P1 scope is what the pilot sites need.

17Application realisation: ideation reference, stack and generation rules

This section is for the team, and for the Singularity code-generation product, that turn this BRD into the live application. Singularity receives two inputs: this BRD and the SynapseDC ideation code (the app/ folder of the ideation repository, live at https://datacenter-ideation.vercel.app). Every screen in Appendix B exists in the ideation code. The generated frontend must look, lay out and behave like it.

17.1How the two inputs divide the work

QuestionSource that winsWhy
What is built, and what is consumed from an existing moduleBRD (3.2, 3.4–3.6, Appendix B status column)The ideation code also holds screens that belong to existing modules. Those are not generated (17.6).
Where data comes from (API, OAP, SynapseDC store)BRD (section 9, 9.11–9.25, Appendix B "Data from")The ideation code serves everything from an in-memory mock.
Layout, components, styling, interaction, states, wording, URL parametersIdeation codeIt is the signed-off UI reference.
Business rules and formulas (impact, money, ride-through, GPU time lost, capacity, power economics, compliance)BRD business rules, implemented as in the ideation read models (app/src/mock/*.ts, app/src/planner/lib/*)The mock read models are the reference implementation of every BRULE.
Field names and shapes on the screen sideIdeation contract app/src/api/types.tsScreens already consume these shapes. API adapters map module responses onto them.
Anything in conflictBRD, then raise the difference (to discuss)—

17.2The ideation code: what each part is and what it becomes

Path in the ideation repoWhat it isIn the generated application
app/src/api/types.tsThe API contract: every shape the screens consumeKeep as the frontend data contract. Generate the OpenAPI document (NB-01) from it.
app/src/api/index.ts, app/src/api/ext-twin.tsThe only module screens import for data: async getX() functions, mutations, useQuery; each function names its future REST path in a commentKeep the signatures. Replace each body with a call to the SynapseDC backend (NB-01 path in the comment). The backend calls the module API (9.11–9.25), OAP, or its own read model and store.
app/src/mock/*Seeded mock estate (seed.ts, generate.ts, personas.ts, geo.ts, incidents.ts, vintage.ts), the mock server (server.ts), and the read modelsNot shipped. The generator, seed and estate ageing are demo data only. The read models become SynapseDC backend services (table 17.5).
app/src/planner/lib/*The planner engine (templates, sizing, cost / NPV / IRR, schedule, rules, governance, sourcing, materials, execute, closeout)Keep as the planner engine. It is datacenter-specific (DC-STORE). It runs in the backend, or client-side over backend data.
app/src/mock/p2b/*, app/src/planner/compat/*Planner record store, adapter from the estate, seed templates and benchmarksThe store becomes the DC-STORE planner tables. The adapter becomes calls to the Inventory, Inventory Planning, IP Management, Supply Chain and Programs & Projects APIs. Templates and benchmarks are seed data for the customer to replace.
app/src/screens/**Every screen and tabGenerate from these for the screens marked Build in Appendix B.
app/src/ui/*The shared UI kit (17.4)Keep and reuse. Never re-implement a control a kit component covers.
app/src/charts/*Hand-built SVG charts: TimeChart, Donut, BarList, Sparkline, HeatGridKeep. No charting library is used.
app/src/shell/*Layout (rail, top bar, breadcrumbs), sub-menus, ⌘K palette, Opstral AI drawer, embed modeKeep. The menu comes from Layout.tsx NAV and submenus.tsx.
app/src/lib/*Formatters (format.ts: money, numbers, dates by geography), vocabulary (vocab.ts), GPU catalogue (gpuSpec.ts), fabric planes, big-screen scaling, forecastKeep.
app/src/styles/*.cssApp CSS: app.css, base.css (shared classes, page rhythm), one file per screen areaKeep. Per-screen CSS does layout only.
app/design-system/*NST / Vision Waves design system (nst-design-system.css, components.css, tokens, themes, fonts, COMPONENTS.md)Keep as is. It is the platform design system.
app/.qa/*Playwright and tsx checks per screen (layout, overflow, data reconciliation)Reuse as acceptance tests (17.8).
app/public/*Static guides (field guide, interface spec, BRD …)Not part of the application.

17.3Technology stack and libraries

The ideation frontend is a single-page React application. It uses no UI component library (no Ant Design, MUI, Bootstrap), no charting library and no state-management library. It also includes no Java library or JAR. The generated frontend shall use the same stack and the same library versions (major and minor):

The backend that serves the SynapseDC API (NB-01), the read models and DC-STORE is not prescribed by the ideation code. It follows the platform's standard service stack (language, framework and any JARs are the platform team's choice; to discuss). It calls the existing modules and the OAP telemetry services only through their APIs (3.4).

17.7Replacing the mock with live data

IDRule
GEN-001Keep every function name, argument and return type in api/index.ts. Only replace the body. Screens must not change because the data source changed.
GEN-002Each body calls the SynapseDC backend at the NB-01 path written next to it (for example getRevenue({ dcId }) → GET /api/dco/v1/revenue-impact?dcId=).
GEN-003The backend serves each call from one of three sources, as Appendix B "Data from" says: an existing module API through an adapter (9.11–9.25), the OAP telemetry consumer API, or a SynapseDC read model over DC-STORE.
GEN-004The read models are ported from the mock files in table 17.5 with the same rules and the same output shapes. The data generation (generate.ts, seed.ts, incidents.ts story data, vintage.ts, the fault injections in gpu.ts) is not ported.
GEN-005onDataChange (the mock's change feed) becomes a server push (SSE or WebSocket) fed by the event bus (NB-02 / NB-03) and the module events (API-…-events). Screens refetch on it, as they do now.
GEN-006Persona (business model) and geography stop regenerating data. They select the vocabulary (mock/personas.ts vocab → configuration), the currency and formats (mock/geo.ts → configuration) and the deployment default / overrides (mock/override.ts → VITE_PERSONA_DEFAULT, VITE_PERSONA_OVERRIDE, VITE_GEO_OVERRIDE).
GEN-007Opstral AI (mock/sentinel.ts, deterministic answers) is replaced by AI Foundry agents (API-AIF-01) that use the SynapseDC API as tools (API-AIF-02). The suggested questions and the deep-link answers stay.
GEN-008Mock latency, structuredClone and the in-memory overlays (dcimCr, IPAM crud, delivery moves, SecOps triage) are removed. Their effects become real writes through the module APIs or DC-STORE.
GEN-009No mock data ships in production. A demo tenant may load the ideation estate through the import template (10.2).

17.8Generation and acceptance rules

IDRule
GEN-010The generated screens shall match the ideation screens at 1280 and 1440 px: same layout, components, order of widgets, wording, empty states, drawers and URL parameters. Reference: the live ideation application with ?persona=colocation or ?persona=neocloud and ?geo=in or ?geo=us.
GEN-011Every screen keeps its URL contract (?tab=, ?gtab=, filters, ?dc=, selected object) so deep links and embeds work. The full list is in the embed guide of the ideation repo (docs/dco-embed-guide.html).
GEN-012The schematic twin (screens/site/twin/*) is signed off. Reproduce it as is: same scene, colours, camera poses and panel.
GEN-013New widgets get an info bubble. New lists use ListToolbar and DataTable. New tabs are added to a sub-menu in shell/submenus.tsx, or they have no way in.
GEN-014Heavy features stay lazy-loaded as in the ideation code: planner routes, the review pack builders (pdfmake, exceljs, fflate), the realistic twin and 3D views.
GEN-015Acceptance: the ideation QA scripts are the reference checks. Port them to the generated application and run them per screen: layout and overflow (grids.mjs, toolbars.mjs, analytics.mjs, tiles2.mjs, sticky.mjs, tabs-survey.mjs, bigscreen.mjs, embed.mjs), screens (cc-hero.mjs, site-sticky.mjs, facilities.mjs, gpu.mjs, revenue.mjs, customer.mjs, capacity.mjs, dcim.mjs, p2b-wizard.mjs, p2b-plans.mjs, admin.mjs, twin2.mjs, devtwin.mjs, map.mjs), and reconciliation (revenue-check.ts, dcim-check.ts, physical-check.ts, access-check.ts, secops-check.ts, cc-numbers.ts), with the data now coming from the live sources.
GEN-016A screen is done when it passes its checks with no horizontal overflow, no cut text, no NaN / undefined and no console errors at 1280 and 1440, and its figures reconcile with the lists they link to (NFR-007).

Appendix BScreen and feature catalogue

This appendix lists, screen by screen and feature by feature, everything SynapseDC builds. A screen is either Build (datacenter-specific), meaning SynapseDC owns the screen and the data behind it, or Build — datacenter view over <module> API, meaning the record stays in the existing module and SynapseDC adds only the datacenter-specific view, calculations and actions. Elements marked link to <module> are not built: the user is taken to the module's own screen. "Data from" names the API endpoints from section 9.11, using these labels:

  • API-…: an existing module's API.
  • API-OAP-…: the OAP telemetry consumer service on ClickHouse.
  • DC-RM <name>: a SynapseDC read model, which calculates results from the APIs.
  • DC-STORE: SynapseDC's own store, which holds datacenter-specific data only.

FR references are to chapters 7.1–7.7 and 7.12. Phases are as in section 12. Colo means colocation only and Neo means NeoCloud only; rows without a mark apply to both.

B.1Command Center

Route / (?lens=build for the build lens) · Menu Overview › Command center · Status Build (datacenter-specific) · Phase P1 (Plan & build strip and lens P2)

This is the landing page: one picture of every datacenter's health, what is happening, who is affected and what to do next.
